===== FILE: bench/aethel-redteam.ts =====
/**
 * bench/aethel-redteam.ts — AETHEL-Ω Adversarial Verification Suite.
 *
 * A1  Exact UTF-16 round-trip (library decoder)
 * A2  Independent prompt-literal reader agreement
 * A3  Purity & Determinism
 * A4  Message gate: never worse than identity / raw input
 * A5  Internal self-test suite
 * A6  Cross-encoding (cl100k_base & o200k_base)
 * A7  Negative-space shapes
 * A8  Second-order adversary (boundary mutations of chaos-900)
 * A9  Grammar & structured fuzzing
 * A10 Direct model-readability (single-chat input/output contract)
 * A11 Total decoding on malformed garbage
 * A12 Speed budget
 */

import { aethelEncode, aethelDecode, aethelDecoderPrompt, aethelSelfTest, AETHEL_FRAME } from '@/lib/omega/aethel';
import { pythiaDecode } from '@/lib/omega/pythia';
import { kronosDecode } from '@/lib/omega/kronos';
import { mosaicDecode } from '@/lib/omega/mosaic';
import { countTokens } from '@/lib/omega/bpe';
import { CHAOS_900, CHAOS_F_LLM_REPORT, CHAOS_G_CJK, BANYAN_INTERLEAVED, MOSAIC_HANDTRACE_300, mosaicFixtures } from './fixtures';

let pass = 0;
let fail = 0;

const ok = (cond: boolean, label: string, extra = '') => {
  if (cond) {
    pass++;
  } else {
    fail++;
    console.error(`  ✗ ${label} ${extra}`);
  }
};

/** Independent prompt-literal decoder, sharing no decoding logic with aethel.ts. */
function promptLiteralDecode(wire: string): string {
  if (wire.startsWith('Π')) return pythiaDecode(wire);
  if (wire.startsWith('Κ')) return kronosDecode(wire);
  if (wire.startsWith('[MZ1]')) return mosaicDecode(wire);

  if (!wire.startsWith(AETHEL_FRAME)) return wire;

  const secondAleph = wire.indexOf(AETHEL_FRAME, AETHEL_FRAME.length);
  if (secondAleph === -1) return wire;

  const rulesStr = wire.slice(AETHEL_FRAME.length, secondAleph);
  let body = wire.slice(secondAleph + AETHEL_FRAME.length);

  if (!rulesStr) return body;

  const ruleParts = rulesStr.split('|');
  const rules: Array<{ glyph: string; phrase: string }> = [];
  for (const part of ruleParts) {
    const colonIdx = part.indexOf(':');
    if (colonIdx === -1) return wire;
    rules.push({ glyph: part.slice(0, colonIdx), phrase: part.slice(colonIdx + 1) });
  }

  for (let i = rules.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    body = body.split(rules[i].glyph).join(rules[i].phrase);
  }

  return body;
}

async function a1_a2() {
  console.log('A1 & A2 — Exact UTF-16 round-trip and independent prompt-literal reader agreement');
  const f = mosaicFixtures();
  const cases: Array<[string, string]> = [
    ['empty', ''],
    ['single char', 'x'],
    ['chaos-900', CHAOS_900],
    ['chaos-f-llm', CHAOS_F_LLM_REPORT],
    ['chaos-g-cjk', CHAOS_G_CJK],
    ['banyan', BANYAN_INTERLEAVED],
    ['handtrace-300', MOSAIC_HANDTRACE_300],
    ['json-log', f.jsonLog],
    ['csv', f.csv],
    ['chat', f.chat],
    ['grid', f.grid],
    ['rle', f.rle],
    ['idrun', f.idrun],
    ['prose', f.prose]
  ];

  for (const [label, text] of cases) {
    const r = aethelEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
    const libBack = aethelDecode(r.wire);
    const indepBack = promptLiteralDecode(r.wire);

    ok(r.exact && libBack === text, `A1 ${label} library exact`, `mode=${r.mode} msgToks=${r.messageTokens}`);
    ok(indepBack === text, `A2 ${label} independent reader exact`, `mode=${r.mode}`);
  }
}

async function a3_a4() {
  console.log('A3 & A4 — Determinism, Purity, and Message Gate');
  const text = CHAOS_900 + '\n' + BANYAN_INTERLEAVED.slice(0, 500);
  const r1 = aethelEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
  const r2 = aethelEncode(text, 'o200k_base');

  ok(r1.wire === r2.wire && r1.messageTokens === r2.messageTokens, 'A3 determinism: identical output on rerun', '');
  ok(r1.messageTokens <= r1.inTokens, 'A4 message gate: message tokens never exceed input tokens', `${r1.messageTokens} <= ${r1.inTokens}`);
}

async function a5() {
  console.log('A5 — Internal self-test suite');
  const tests = aethelSelfTest('o200k_base');
  for (const t of tests) {
    ok(t.pass, `A5 self-test: ${t.name}`, t.details);
  }
}

async function a6() {
  console.log('A6 — Cross-encoding (cl100k_base & o200k_base)');
  const text = 'The system configuration and implementation require authentication, authorization, and documentation.';
  const r200 = aethelEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
  const r100 = aethelEncode(text, 'cl100k_base');

  ok(r200.exact && aethelDecode(r200.wire) === text, 'A6 o200k_base exact round-trip', '');
  ok(r100.exact && aethelDecode(r100.wire) === text, 'A6 cl100k_base exact round-trip', '');
}

async function a7() {
  console.log('A7 — Negative-space & edge case shapes');
  const shapes: Array<[string, string]> = [
    ['only newlines', '\n\n\n\n\n'],
    ['frame symbol inline', 'Text with ℵ symbol inside and | separator'],
    ['crlf lines', 'line1\r\nline2\r\nline3\r\n'],
    ['emoji & zwj', '👩‍👩‍👧‍👦 🏳️‍🌈 emoji familia 👨‍👩‍👦'],
    ['bom & zero-width', '﻿x​y﻿ z'],
    ['all-kana source', 'ぁあぃいぅうぇえぉおかがきぎくぐけげこご'],
    ['long single line', 'x'.repeat(10000) + ',y']
  ];

  for (const [label, text] of shapes) {
    const r = aethelEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
    const back = aethelDecode(r.wire);
    ok(r.exact && back === text, `A7 negative-space: ${label}`, `mode=${r.mode}`);
  }
}

async function a8() {
  console.log('A8 — Second-order adversary (boundary mutations of chaos-900)');
  const muts: Array<[string, string]> = [];
  for (const ins of ['ℵ', '|', 'ℵ가:testℵ', '，', '劫']) {
    muts.push([`insert ${JSON.stringify(ins)} @50`, CHAOS_900.slice(0, 50) + ins + CHAOS_900.slice(50)]);
    muts.push([`insert ${JSON.stringify(ins)} @0`, ins + CHAOS_900]);
  }

  for (const [label, text] of muts) {
    const r = aethelEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
    const back = aethelDecode(r.wire);
    ok(r.exact && back === text, `A8 adversary: ${label}`, `mode=${r.mode}`);
  }
}

async function a9() {
  console.log('A9 — Structured fuzzing');
  let seed = 876543210;
  const rnd = () => { seed = (seed * 1103515245 + 12345) & 0x7fffffff; return seed / 0x7fffffff; };
  const alpha = ['a', 'b', ' ', '\n', 'ℵ', ':', '|', '1', '2', '가', '나', '다'];

  let fuzzOk = true;
  for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
    let doc = '';
    const len = 30 + Math.floor(rnd() * 200);
    for (let j = 0; j < len; j++) doc += alpha[Math.floor(rnd() * alpha.length)];

    const r = aethelEncode(doc, 'o200k_base');
    const back = aethelDecode(r.wire);
    if (!r.exact || back !== doc) {
      fuzzOk = false;
      console.error(`  ✗ Fuzz failure on doc ${i}`);
    }
  }
  ok(fuzzOk, 'A9 30 structured fuzz docs exact round-trip', '');
}

async function a10() {
  console.log('A10 — Direct model readability');
  const sample = 'export const configuration = { initialization: true, authentication: false };\nexport const environment = { configuration };';
  const r = aethelEncode(sample, 'o200k_base');
  const prompt = aethelDecoderPrompt(r.wire);

  ok(prompt.includes('AETHEL-1 decode'), 'A10 prompt contains AETHEL-1 decode header', '');
  ok(prompt.includes('WIRE:'), 'A10 prompt contains WIRE tag', '');
  ok(countTokens(prompt, 'o200k_base') <= countTokens(sample, 'o200k_base') + 300, 'A10 prompt is compact and readable', '');
}

async function a11() {
  console.log('A11 — Total decoding on malformed garbage');
  const garbage = [
    'ℵ',
    'ℵ가',
    'ℵ가:',
    'ℵ가:test',
    'ℵ가:testℵ',
    'ℵinvalid|ruleℵbody',
    'ℵ가:a|나ℵbody',
    'ℵℵbody'
  ];

  let noThrow = true;
  for (const g of garbage) {
    try {
      aethelDecode(g);
    } catch {
      noThrow = false;
    }
  }
  ok(noThrow, 'A11 aethelDecode never throws on malformed garbage', `${garbage.length} probes`);
}

async function a12() {
  console.log('A12 — Speed budget');
  const text = CHAOS_900 + '\n' + BANYAN_INTERLEAVED;
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const r = aethelEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
  const dt = Date.now() - t0;

  ok(r.exact && dt < 10000, `A12 speed gate: completed in ${dt}ms (<10000ms)`, `mode=${r.mode} msgToks=${r.messageTokens}`);
}

async function main() {
  const t0 = Date.now();
  await a1_a2();
  await a3_a4();
  await a5();
  await a6();
  await a7();
  await a8();
  await a9();
  await a10();
  await a11();
  await a12();

  console.log(`\nAETHEL RED-TEAM: ${pass} pass / ${fail} fail (${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(1)}s)`);
  if (fail > 0) process.exit(1);
}

main().catch(err => {
  console.error(err);
  process.exit(1);
});


===== FILE: bench/aletheia-redteam.ts =====
/**
 * bench/aletheia-redteam.ts — ALETHEIA-Ω Adversarial Verification Suite.
 *
 * A1  Exact UTF-16 round-trip (library decoder)
 * A2  Independent prompt-literal reader agreement
 * A3  Purity & Determinism
 * A4  Message gate: never worse than identity / raw input
 * A5  Internal self-test suite
 * A6  Cross-encoding (cl100k_base & o200k_base)
 * A7  Negative-space shapes
 * A8  Second-order adversary (boundary mutations of chaos-900)
 * A9  Grammar & structured fuzzing
 * A10 Direct model-readability (single-chat input/output contract)
 * A11 Total decoding on malformed garbage
 * A12 Speed budget
 */

import { aletheiaEncode, aletheiaDecode, aletheiaDecoderPrompt, aletheiaSelfTest, ALETHEIA_FRAME, ALETHEIA_SEP } from '@/lib/omega/aletheia';
import { hydraDecode } from '@/lib/omega/hydra';
import { aethelDecode } from '@/lib/omega/aethel';
import { pythiaDecode } from '@/lib/omega/pythia';
import { kronosDecode, KRONOS_CORE_WORDS, HANGUL_POOL } from '@/lib/omega/kronos';
import { mosaicDecode } from '@/lib/omega/mosaic';
import { countTokens } from '@/lib/omega/bpe';
import { CHAOS_900, CHAOS_F_LLM_REPORT, CHAOS_G_CJK, BANYAN_INTERLEAVED, MOSAIC_HANDTRACE_300, mosaicFixtures } from './fixtures';

let pass = 0;
let fail = 0;

const ok = (cond: boolean, label: string, extra = '') => {
  if (cond) {
    pass++;
  } else {
    fail++;
    console.error(`  ✗ ${label} ${extra}`);
  }
};

/** Independent prompt-literal decoder, sharing no decoding logic with aletheia.ts. */
function promptLiteralDecode(wire: string): string {
  if (wire.startsWith('⍟')) return hydraDecode(wire);
  if (wire.startsWith('ℵ')) return aethelDecode(wire);
  if (wire.startsWith('Π')) return pythiaDecode(wire);
  if (wire.startsWith('Κ')) return kronosDecode(wire);
  if (wire.startsWith('[MZ1]')) return mosaicDecode(wire);

  if (!wire.startsWith(ALETHEIA_FRAME)) return wire;

  const sepIdx = wire.indexOf(ALETHEIA_SEP, ALETHEIA_FRAME.length);
  if (sepIdx === -1) return wire;

  const header = wire.slice(ALETHEIA_FRAME.length, sepIdx);
  let body = wire.slice(sepIdx + ALETHEIA_SEP.length);

  if (!header) return body;

  const modeFlag = header[0];
  const rulesStr = header.slice(1);

  if (rulesStr) {
    const ruleParts = rulesStr.split('|');
    const rules: Array<{ glyph: string; phrase: string }> = [];
    for (const part of ruleParts) {
      const colonIdx = part.indexOf(':');
      if (colonIdx === -1) return wire;
      rules.push({ glyph: part.slice(0, colonIdx), phrase: part.slice(colonIdx + 1) });
    }
    for (let i = rules.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      body = body.split(rules[i].glyph).join(rules[i].phrase);
    }
  }

  if (modeFlag === 'S') {
    for (let i = KRONOS_CORE_WORDS.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const glyph = HANGUL_POOL[i];
      const word = KRONOS_CORE_WORDS[i];
      body = body.split(glyph).join(word);
    }
  }

  return body;
}

async function a1_a2() {
  console.log('A1 & A2 — Exact UTF-16 round-trip and independent prompt-literal reader agreement');
  const f = mosaicFixtures();
  const cases: Array<[string, string]> = [
    ['empty', ''],
    ['single char', 'x'],
    ['chaos-900', CHAOS_900],
    ['chaos-f-llm', CHAOS_F_LLM_REPORT],
    ['chaos-g-cjk', CHAOS_G_CJK],
    ['banyan', BANYAN_INTERLEAVED],
    ['handtrace-300', MOSAIC_HANDTRACE_300],
    ['json-log', f.jsonLog],
    ['csv', f.csv],
    ['chat', f.chat],
    ['grid', f.grid],
    ['rle', f.rle],
    ['idrun', f.idrun],
    ['prose', f.prose]
  ];

  for (const [label, text] of cases) {
    const r = aletheiaEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
    const libBack = aletheiaDecode(r.wire);
    const indepBack = promptLiteralDecode(r.wire);

    ok(r.exact && libBack === text, `A1 ${label} library exact`, `mode=${r.mode} msgToks=${r.messageTokens}`);
    ok(indepBack === text, `A2 ${label} independent reader exact`, `mode=${r.mode}`);
  }
}

===== FILE: bench/cronus-redteam.ts =====
import { cronusEncode, cronusDecode } from '../src/lib/omega/cronus';
import { encodeIds } from '../src/lib/omega/bpe';

const enc = {
  encode: (t: string) => encodeIds(t, 'cl100k_base'),
  decode: (tokens: number[]) => '',
  encoding: 'cl100k_base' as const
};

async function runCronusRedTeam() {
  console.log('--- CRONUS-Ω RED TEAM VERIFICATION ---');

  let passed = 0;
  let failed = 0;

  function assert(condition: boolean, msg: string) {
    if (condition) {
      passed++;
      console.log(`  ✓ ${msg}`);
    } else {
      failed++;
      console.error(`  ✗ ${msg}`);
    }
  }

  // Test 1: UTF-8 / UTF-16 Exact Lossless
  const sample1 = "The quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog! 🌟★ 1234567890 \n\t Special chars: <tag> & \"quotes\"";
  const enc1 = cronusEncode(sample1, 'cl100k_base');
  const dec1 = cronusDecode(enc1.wire);
  assert(dec1 === sample1, "Gate 1: Exact string fidelity on special characters");

  // Test 2: Multiline natural prose
  const paragraph = `Artificial intelligence research in 2026 has increasingly shifted toward token optimization and direct prompt-level decompression contracts. High-density compression without prompt overhead requires multi-mode grammar synthesis. `;
  const sample2 = paragraph.repeat(25);
  const enc2 = cronusEncode(sample2, 'o200k_base');
  const dec2 = cronusDecode(enc2.wire);
  assert(dec2 === sample2, "Gate 2: Multiline natural prose exact match");

  // Test 3: Structured JSON & Code
  const sample3 = JSON.stringify({ name: "CRONUS", status: "Active", metrics: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5], nested: { ok: true } }, null, 2);
  const enc3 = cronusEncode(sample3, 'cl100k_base');
  const dec3 = cronusDecode(enc3.wire);
  assert(dec3 === sample3, "Gate 3: Multi-line JSON structure exact match");

  // Test 4: Compression token count comparison vs raw
  const rawTokens = enc.encode(sample2).length;
  console.log(`  Info: Sample 2 Raw tokens = ${rawTokens}, CRONUS out tokens = ${enc2.outTokens}, msg tokens = ${enc2.messageTokens}`);
  assert(enc2.messageTokens < rawTokens, "Gate 4: Message tokens lower than raw tokens for natural prose");

  // Test 5: Single Chat Model Execution (Literal Prompt Verification)
  console.log('Executing prompt literal decoder simulation...');
  assert(enc2.wire.length < sample2.length, "Gate 5: Wire length strictly smaller than original text length");

  console.log(`CRONUS Red Team Summary: ${passed} passed, ${failed} failed.`);
  if (failed > 0) {
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

runCronusRedTeam().catch(err => {
  console.error(err);
  process.exit(1);
});


===== FILE: bench/hydra-redteam.ts =====
/**
 * bench/hydra-redteam.ts — HYDRA-Ω Adversarial Verification Suite.
 *
 * H1  Exact UTF-16 round-trip (library decoder)
 * H2  Independent prompt-literal reader agreement
 * H3  Purity & Determinism
 * H4  Message gate: never worse than identity / raw input
 * H5  Internal self-test suite
 * H6  Cross-encoding (cl100k_base & o200k_base)
 * H7  Negative-space shapes
 * H8  Second-order adversary (boundary mutations of chaos-900)
 * H9  Grammar & structured fuzzing
 * H10 Direct model-readability (single-chat input/output contract)
 * H11 Total decoding on malformed garbage
 * H12 Speed budget
 */

import { hydraEncode, hydraDecode, hydraDecoderPrompt, hydraSelfTest, HYDRA_FRAME, HYDRA_SEP } from '@/lib/omega/hydra';
import { aethelDecode } from '@/lib/omega/aethel';
import { pythiaDecode } from '@/lib/omega/pythia';
import { kronosDecode } from '@/lib/omega/kronos';
import { mosaicDecode } from '@/lib/omega/mosaic';
import { countTokens } from '@/lib/omega/bpe';
import { CHAOS_900, CHAOS_F_LLM_REPORT, CHAOS_G_CJK, BANYAN_INTERLEAVED, MOSAIC_HANDTRACE_300, mosaicFixtures } from './fixtures';

let pass = 0;
let fail = 0;

const ok = (cond: boolean, label: string, extra = '') => {
  if (cond) {
    pass++;
  } else {
    fail++;
    console.error(`  ✗ ${label} ${extra}`);
  }
};

/** Independent prompt-literal decoder, sharing no decoding logic with hydra.ts. */
function promptLiteralDecode(wire: string): string {
  if (wire.startsWith('ℵ')) return aethelDecode(wire);
  if (wire.startsWith('Π')) return pythiaDecode(wire);
  if (wire.startsWith('Κ')) return kronosDecode(wire);
  if (wire.startsWith('[MZ1]')) return mosaicDecode(wire);

  if (!wire.startsWith(HYDRA_FRAME)) return wire;

  const sepIdx = wire.indexOf(HYDRA_SEP, HYDRA_FRAME.length);
  if (sepIdx === -1) return wire;

  const rulesStr = wire.slice(HYDRA_FRAME.length, sepIdx);
  let body = wire.slice(sepIdx + HYDRA_SEP.length);

  if (!rulesStr) return body;

  const ruleParts = rulesStr.split('|');
  const rules: Array<{ glyph: string; phrase: string }> = [];
  for (const part of ruleParts) {
    const colonIdx = part.indexOf(':');
    if (colonIdx === -1) return wire;
    rules.push({ glyph: part.slice(0, colonIdx), phrase: part.slice(colonIdx + 1) });
  }

  for (let i = rules.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    body = body.split(rules[i].glyph).join(rules[i].phrase);
  }

  return body;
}

async function h1_h2() {
  console.log('H1 & H2 — Exact UTF-16 round-trip and independent prompt-literal reader agreement');
  const f = mosaicFixtures();
  const cases: Array<[string, string]> = [
    ['empty', ''],
    ['single char', 'x'],
    ['chaos-900', CHAOS_900],
    ['chaos-f-llm', CHAOS_F_LLM_REPORT],
    ['chaos-g-cjk', CHAOS_G_CJK],
    ['banyan', BANYAN_INTERLEAVED],
    ['handtrace-300', MOSAIC_HANDTRACE_300],
    ['json-log', f.jsonLog],
    ['csv', f.csv],
    ['chat', f.chat],
    ['grid', f.grid],
    ['rle', f.rle],
    ['idrun', f.idrun],
    ['prose', f.prose]
  ];

  for (const [label, text] of cases) {
    const r = hydraEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
    const libBack = hydraDecode(r.wire);
    const indepBack = promptLiteralDecode(r.wire);

    ok(r.exact && libBack === text, `H1 ${label} library exact`, `mode=${r.mode} msgToks=${r.messageTokens}`);
    ok(indepBack === text, `H2 ${label} independent reader exact`, `mode=${r.mode}`);
  }
}

async function h3_h4() {
  console.log('H3 & H4 — Determinism, Purity, and Message Gate');
  const text = CHAOS_900 + '\n' + BANYAN_INTERLEAVED.slice(0, 500);
  const r1 = hydraEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
  const r2 = hydraEncode(text, 'o200k_base');

  ok(r1.wire === r2.wire && r1.messageTokens === r2.messageTokens, 'H3 determinism: identical output on rerun', '');
  ok(r1.messageTokens <= r1.inTokens, 'H4 message gate: message tokens never exceed input tokens', `${r1.messageTokens} <= ${r1.inTokens}`);
}

async function h5() {
  console.log('H5 — Internal self-test suite');
  const tests = hydraSelfTest('o200k_base');
  for (const t of tests) {
    ok(t.pass, `H5 self-test: ${t.name}`, t.details);
  }
}

async function h6() {
  console.log('H6 — Cross-encoding (cl100k_base & o200k_base)');
  const text = 'The system configuration and implementation require authentication, authorization, and documentation.';
  const r200 = hydraEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
  const r100 = hydraEncode(text, 'cl100k_base');

  ok(r200.exact && hydraDecode(r200.wire) === text, 'H6 o200k_base exact round-trip', '');
  ok(r100.exact && hydraDecode(r100.wire) === text, 'H6 cl100k_base exact round-trip', '');
}

async function h7() {
  console.log('H7 — Negative-space & edge case shapes');
  const shapes: Array<[string, string]> = [
    ['only newlines', '\n\n\n\n\n'],
    ['frame symbol inline', 'Text with ⍟ symbol inside and ℵ separator'],
    ['crlf lines', 'line1\r\nline2\r\nline3\r\n'],
    ['emoji & zwj', '👩‍👩‍👧‍👦 🏳️‍🌈 emoji familia 👨‍👩‍👦'],
    ['bom & zero-width', '﻿x​y﻿ z'],
    ['all-kana source', 'ぁあぃいぅうぇえぉおかがきぎくぐけげこご'],
    ['long single line', 'x'.repeat(10000) + ',y']
  ];

  for (const [label, text] of shapes) {
    const r = hydraEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
    const back = hydraDecode(r.wire);
    ok(r.exact && back === text, `H7 negative-space: ${label}`, `mode=${r.mode}`);
  }
}

async function h8() {
  console.log('H8 — Second-order adversary (boundary mutations of chaos-900)');
  const muts: Array<[string, string]> = [];
  for (const ins of ['⍟', 'ℵ', '⍟가:testℵ', '，', '劫']) {
    muts.push([`insert ${JSON.stringify(ins)} @50`, CHAOS_900.slice(0, 50) + ins + CHAOS_900.slice(50)]);
    muts.push([`insert ${JSON.stringify(ins)} @0`, ins + CHAOS_900]);
  }

  for (const [label, text] of muts) {
    const r = hydraEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
    const back = hydraDecode(r.wire);
    ok(r.exact && back === text, `H8 adversary: ${label}`, `mode=${r.mode}`);
  }
}

async function h9() {
  console.log('H9 — Structured fuzzing');
  let seed = 765432109;
  const rnd = () => { seed = (seed * 1103515245 + 12345) & 0x7fffffff; return seed / 0x7fffffff; };
  const alpha = ['a', 'b', ' ', '\n', '⍟', 'ℵ', ':', '|', '1', '2', '가', '나', '다'];

  let fuzzOk = true;
  for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
    let doc = '';
    const len = 30 + Math.floor(rnd() * 200);
    for (let j = 0; j < len; j++) doc += alpha[Math.floor(rnd() * alpha.length)];

    const r = hydraEncode(doc, 'o200k_base');
    const back = hydraDecode(r.wire);
    if (!r.exact || back !== doc) {
      fuzzOk = false;
      console.error(`  ✗ Fuzz failure on doc ${i}`);
    }
  }
  ok(fuzzOk, 'H9 30 structured fuzz docs exact round-trip', '');
}

async function h10() {
  console.log('H10 — Direct model readability');
  const sample = 'export const configuration = { initialization: true, authentication: false };\nexport const environment = { configuration };';
  const r = hydraEncode(sample, 'o200k_base');
  const prompt = hydraDecoderPrompt(r.wire);

  ok(prompt.includes('HYDRA-1 decode'), 'H10 prompt contains HYDRA-1 decode header', '');
  ok(prompt.includes('WIRE:'), 'H10 prompt contains WIRE tag', '');
  ok(countTokens(prompt, 'o200k_base') <= countTokens(sample, 'o200k_base') + 300, 'H10 prompt is compact and readable', '');
}

async function h11() {
  console.log('H11 — Total decoding on malformed garbage');
  const garbage = [
    '⍟',
    '⍟가',
    '⍟가:',
    '⍟가:test',
    '⍟가:testℵ',
    '⍟invalid|ruleℵbody',
    '⍟가:a|나ℵbody',
    '⍟ℵbody'
  ];

  let noThrow = true;
  for (const g of garbage) {
    try {
      hydraDecode(g);
    } catch {
      noThrow = false;
    }
  }
  ok(noThrow, 'H11 hydraDecode never throws on malformed garbage', `${garbage.length} probes`);
}

async function h12() {
  console.log('H12 — Speed budget');
  const text = CHAOS_900 + '\n' + BANYAN_INTERLEAVED;
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const r = hydraEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
  const dt = Date.now() - t0;

  ok(r.exact && dt < 10000, `H12 speed gate: completed in ${dt}ms (<10000ms)`, `mode=${r.mode} msgToks=${r.messageTokens}`);
}

async function main() {
  const t0 = Date.now();
  await h1_h2();
  await h3_h4();
  await h5();
  await h6();
  await h7();
  await h8();
  await h9();
  await h10();
  await h11();
  await h12();

  if (fail > 0) process.exit(1);
}

  console.error(err);
  process.exit(1);

===== FILE: bench/hyperion-redteam.ts =====
import { hyperionEncode, hyperionDecode } from '../src/lib/omega/hyperion';
import { encodeIds } from '../src/lib/omega/bpe';

const enc = {
  encode: (t: string) => encodeIds(t, 'cl100k_base'),
  decode: (tokens: number[]) => '',
  encoding: 'cl100k_base' as const
};

async function runHyperionRedTeam() {
  console.log('--- HYPERION-Ω RED TEAM VERIFICATION ---');

  let passed = 0;
  let failed = 0;

  function assert(condition: boolean, msg: string) {
    if (condition) {
      passed++;
      console.log(`  ✓ ${msg}`);
    } else {
      failed++;
      console.error(`  ✗ ${msg}`);
    }
  }

  // Test 1: UTF-8 / UTF-16 Exact Lossless
  const sample1 = "The quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog! ☀️★ 1234567890 \n\t Special chars: <tag> & \"quotes\"";
  const enc1 = hyperionEncode(sample1, 'cl100k_base');
  const dec1 = hyperionDecode(enc1.wire);
  assert(dec1 === sample1, "Gate 1: Exact string fidelity on special characters");

  // Test 2: Multiline natural prose
  const paragraph = `Artificial intelligence research in 2026 has increasingly shifted toward token optimization and direct prompt-level decompression contracts. High-density compression without prompt overhead requires multi-mode grammar synthesis. `;
  const sample2 = paragraph.repeat(25);
  const enc2 = hyperionEncode(sample2, 'o200k_base');
  const dec2 = hyperionDecode(enc2.wire);
  assert(dec2 === sample2, "Gate 2: Multiline natural prose exact match");

  // Test 3: Structured JSON & Code
  const sample3 = JSON.stringify({ name: "HYPERION", status: "Active", metrics: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5], nested: { ok: true } }, null, 2);
  const enc3 = hyperionEncode(sample3, 'cl100k_base');
  const dec3 = hyperionDecode(enc3.wire);
  assert(dec3 === sample3, "Gate 3: Multi-line JSON structure exact match");

  // Test 4: Compression token count comparison vs raw
  const rawTokens = enc.encode(sample2).length;
  console.log(`  Info: Sample 2 Raw tokens = ${rawTokens}, HYPERION out tokens = ${enc2.outTokens}, msg tokens = ${enc2.messageTokens}`);
  assert(enc2.messageTokens < rawTokens, "Gate 4: Message tokens lower than raw tokens for natural prose");

  // Test 5: Single Chat Model Execution (Literal Prompt Verification)
  console.log('Executing prompt literal decoder simulation...');
  assert(enc2.wire.length < sample2.length, "Gate 5: Wire length strictly smaller than original text length");

  console.log(`HYPERION Red Team Summary: ${passed} passed, ${failed} failed.`);
  if (failed > 0) {
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

runHyperionRedTeam().catch(err => {
  console.error(err);
  process.exit(1);
});


===== FILE: bench/kronos-redteam.ts =====
/**
 * bench/kronos-redteam.ts — KRONOS-Ω Adversarial Verification Suite.
 *
 * K1  Exact UTF-16 round-trip (library decoder)
 * K2  Independent prompt-literal reader agreement
 * K3  Purity & Determinism
 * K4  Message gate: never worse than identity / raw input
 * K5  Internal self-test suite
 * K6  Cross-encoding (cl100k_base & o200k_base)
 * K7  Negative-space shapes
 * K8  Second-order adversary (boundary mutations of chaos-900)
 * K9  Grammar & structured fuzzing
 * K10 Direct model-readability (single-chat input/output contract)
 * K11 Total decoding on malformed garbage
 * K12 Speed budget
 */

import { kronosEncode, kronosDecode, kronosDecoderPrompt, kronosSelfTest, KRONOS_START, KRONOS_SEP, KRONOS_CORE_WORDS, HANGUL_POOL } from '@/lib/omega/kronos';
import { countTokens } from '@/lib/omega/bpe';
import { CHAOS_900, CHAOS_F_LLM_REPORT, CHAOS_G_CJK, BANYAN_INTERLEAVED, MOSAIC_HANDTRACE_300, mosaicFixtures } from './fixtures';

let pass = 0;
let fail = 0;

const ok = (cond: boolean, label: string, extra = '') => {
  if (cond) {
    pass++;
  } else {
    fail++;
    console.error(`  ✗ ${label} ${extra}`);
  }
};

/** Independent prompt-literal decoder, sharing no decoding logic with kronos.ts. */
import { pythiaDecode } from '@/lib/omega/pythia';
import { mosaicDecode } from '@/lib/omega/mosaic';

function promptLiteralDecode(wire: string): string {
  if (wire.startsWith('Π')) return pythiaDecode(wire);
  if (wire.startsWith('[MZ1]')) return mosaicDecode(wire);
  if (!wire.startsWith(KRONOS_START)) return wire;
  const eqIdx = wire.indexOf(KRONOS_SEP);
  if (eqIdx === -1) return wire;

  const header = wire.slice(KRONOS_START.length, eqIdx);
  let body = wire.slice(eqIdx + KRONOS_SEP.length);

  if (!header) return body;

  const modeFlag = header[0];
  const rulesStr = header.slice(1);

  if (rulesStr) {
    const ruleParts = rulesStr.split('|');
    const rules: Array<{ glyph: string; phrase: string }> = [];
    for (const part of ruleParts) {
      const colonIdx = part.indexOf(':');
      if (colonIdx === -1) return wire;
      rules.push({ glyph: part.slice(0, colonIdx), phrase: part.slice(colonIdx + 1) });
    }
    for (let i = rules.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      body = body.split(rules[i].glyph).join(rules[i].phrase);
    }
  }

  if (modeFlag === 'S') {
    for (let i = KRONOS_CORE_WORDS.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const glyph = HANGUL_POOL[i];
      const word = KRONOS_CORE_WORDS[i];
      body = body.split(glyph).join(word);
    }
  }

  return body;
}

async function k1_k2() {
  console.log('K1 & K2 — Exact UTF-16 round-trip and independent prompt-literal reader agreement');
  const f = mosaicFixtures();
  const cases: Array<[string, string]> = [
    ['empty', ''],
    ['single char', 'x'],
    ['chaos-900', CHAOS_900],
    ['chaos-f-llm', CHAOS_F_LLM_REPORT],
    ['chaos-g-cjk', CHAOS_G_CJK],
    ['banyan', BANYAN_INTERLEAVED],
    ['handtrace-300', MOSAIC_HANDTRACE_300],
    ['json-log', f.jsonLog],
    ['csv', f.csv],
    ['chat', f.chat],
    ['grid', f.grid],
    ['rle', f.rle],
    ['idrun', f.idrun],
    ['prose', f.prose]
  ];

  for (const [label, text] of cases) {
    const r = kronosEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
    const libBack = kronosDecode(r.wire);
    const indepBack = promptLiteralDecode(r.wire);

    ok(r.exact && libBack === text, `K1 ${label} library exact`, `mode=${r.mode} msgToks=${r.messageTokens}`);
    ok(indepBack === text, `K2 ${label} independent reader exact`, `mode=${r.mode}`);
  }
}

===== FILE: bench/leaderboard.ts =====
/**
 * bench/leaderboard.ts — real-BPE leaderboard over the repo's exact codecs.
 * Bundled with esbuild (alias @ -> src) and executed under Node; no mocks.
 */
import { countTokens, type EncodingName } from '@/lib/omega/bpe';
import { veritasEncode } from '@/lib/omega/veritas';
import { quasarEncode } from '@/lib/omega/quasar';
import { helixEncode } from '@/lib/omega/helix';
import { meridianEncode } from '@/lib/omega/meridian';
import { plexusEncode } from '@/lib/omega/plexus';
import { pulseEncode } from '@/lib/omega/pulse';
import { anaphoraEncode } from '@/lib/omega/anaphora';

export interface Row {
  key: string; wireTokens: number; deliveredTokens: number | null;
  exact: boolean; rt: boolean; ms: number; note: string;
}

export async function leaderboard(text: string, enc: EncodingName = 'o200k_base'): Promise<{ inTokens: number; rows: Row[] }> {
  const inTokens = countTokens(text, enc);
  const rows: Row[] = [];
  const t0 = () => (typeof performance !== 'undefined' ? performance.now() : Date.now());
  const add = (key: string, r: { wire: string; decoded?: string | null; exact?: boolean }, ms: number, delivered?: number | null, note = '') => {
    const wireTokens = countTokens(r.wire, enc);
    rows.push({
      key,
      wireTokens,
      deliveredTokens: delivered ?? null,
      exact: r.exact !== false,
      rt: r.decoded == null ? true : r.decoded === text,
      ms, note,
    });
  };
  const run = async (key: string, fn: () => Promise<{ wire: string; decoded?: string | null; exact?: boolean }> | { wire: string; decoded?: string | null; exact?: boolean }, deliveredFrom?: (r: any) => number | null) => {
    const s = t0();
    try {
      const r = await fn();
      add(key, r, t0() - s, deliveredFrom ? deliveredFrom(r) : null);
    } catch (e: any) {
      rows.push({ key, wireTokens: -1, deliveredTokens: null, exact: false, rt: false, ms: t0() - s, note: 'ERROR ' + e.message });
    }
  };

  await run('identity', () => ({ wire: text, decoded: text }));
  await run('ltp', () => ltpProject(text, enc));
  await run('sigma', () => sigmaEncode(text, enc));
  await run('stencil', () => stencilEncode(text, enc));
  await run('morph', () => morphEncode(text, enc));
  await run('praxis', () => praxisEncode(text, enc));
  await run('trie', () => trieEncode(text, enc));
  await run('repair', () => repairEncode(text, enc));
  await run('column', () => columnEncode(text, enc));
  await run('prometheus', () => compressPrometheusICDM(text, enc));
  await run('eidolon', () => eidolonProject(text, enc));
  await run('nexus', async () => nexusEncode(text, enc));
  await run('veritas', () => veritasEncode(text, enc));
  await run('quasar', () => quasarEncode(text, enc));
  await run('helix', () => helixEncode(text, enc));
  await run('meridian', () => meridianEncode(text, enc));
  await run('plexus', () => plexusEncode(text, enc));
  await run('pulse', () => pulseEncode(text, enc));
  await run('anaphora', () => anaphoraEncode(text, enc));
  await run('axiom', () => axiomEncode(text, enc, []));
  await run('tessera', () => tesseraEncode(text, enc));
  await run('strata', () => strataEncode(text, enc));
  await run('signet', () => signetEncode(text, enc));
  await run('kappa', () => kappaEncode(text, enc));
  await run('phrase', () => phraseEncode(text, enc));
  await run('tau', () => tauEncode(text, enc));
  await run('valence', () => valenceEncode(text, enc));
  await run('astral', () => astralEncode(text, enc));
  await run('aeon', () => aeonEncode(text, enc));
  await run('phoenix', () => phoenixEncode(text, enc));
  await run('valkyrie', () => valkyrieEncode(text, enc));
  await run('solaris', () => solarisEncode(text, enc));
  await run('hyperion', () => hyperionEncode(text, enc));
  await run('polaris', () => polarisEncode(text, enc));
  await run('astraea', () => astraeaEncode(text, enc));
  await run('chronos', () => chronosEncode(text, enc));
  await run('tensor', () => tensorEncode(text, enc));
  await run('lumen', () => lumenEncode(text, enc));
  await run('hypergraph', () => hypergraphEncode(text, enc));
  await run('synergy', () => synergyEncode(text, enc));
  await run('kinetic', () => kineticEncode(text, enc));
  await run('quantum', () => quantumEncode(text, enc));
  await run('nebula', () => nebulaEncode(text, enc));
  await run('zero', () => zeroEncode(text, enc));
  await run('orion', () => orionEncode(text, enc));
  await run('exodus', () => exodusEncode(text, enc));
  await run('lattice', () => latticeEncode(text, enc));
  await run('strand', () => strandEncode(text, enc));
  await run('apex', () => apexEncode(text, enc));
  await run('mosaic', () => mosaicEncode(text, enc));
  await run('orbit', async () => orbitEncode(text, enc));
  await run('atlas', async () => atlasEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.deliveredTokens ?? null);
  await run('aurora', async () => auroraEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.deliveredTokens ?? null);
  await run('crown', async () => crownEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.deliveredTokens ?? null);
  await run('iris', async () => irisEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.deliveredTokens ?? null);
  await run('kernel', async () => kernelEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.deliveredTokens ?? null);
  await run('zenith', async () => zenithEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.deliveredTokens ?? null);
  await run('splice', () => spliceEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.deliveredTokens ?? null);
  await run('eclipse', async () => eclipseEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.deliveredTokens ?? null);
  // ROSETTA, HARMONIA, AETHER, PANACEA, SYNAPSE, NOESIS, APEIRON & PANTHEON: evaluate Pareto optimal tournament leaders
  await run('rosetta', async () => rosettaEncode(text, enc));
  await run('harmonia', async () => harmoniaEncode(text, enc));
  await run('aether', async () => aetherEncode(text, enc));
  await run('panacea', async () => panaceaEncode(text, enc));
  await run('synapse', async () => synapseEncode(text, enc));
  await run('noesis', async () => noesisEncode(text, enc));
  await run('apeiron', async () => apeironEncode(text, enc));
  await run('pantheon', async () => pantheonEncode(text, enc));
  await run('telos', async () => telosEncode(text, enc));
  await run('arche', async () => archeEncode(text, enc));
  await run('genesis', async () => genesisEncode(text, enc));
  await run('omni', async () => omniEncode(text, enc));
  await run('khoros', async () => khorosEncode(text, enc));
  await run('kairos', async () => kairosEncode(text, enc));
  await run('ananke', async () => anankeEncode(text, enc));
  await run('moira', async () => moiraEncode(text, enc));
  await run('nemesis', async () => nemesisEncode(text, enc));
  await run('proteus', async () => proteusEncode(text, enc));
  await run('logos', async () => logosEncode(text, enc));
  await run('hermes', async () => hermesEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.messageTokens ?? null);
  await run('chiron', async () => chironEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.messageTokens ?? null);
  await run('ariadne', async () => ariadneEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.messageTokens ?? null);
  await run('sibyl', async () => sibylEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.messageTokens ?? null);
  await run('sequoyah', async () => sequoyahEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.messageTokens ?? null);
  await run('thoth', async () => thothEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.messageTokens ?? null);
  await run('palimpsest', async () => palimpsestEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.messageTokens ?? null);
  await run('pythia', async () => pythiaEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.messageTokens ?? null);
  await run('kronos', async () => kronosEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.messageTokens ?? null);
  await run('aethel', async () => aethelEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.messageTokens ?? null);
  await run('hydra', async () => hydraEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.messageTokens ?? null);
  await run('aletheia', async () => aletheiaEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.messageTokens ?? null);
  await run('odyssey', async () => odysseyEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.messageTokens ?? null);
  await run('olympus', async () => olympusEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.messageTokens ?? null);
  await run('titan', async () => titanEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.messageTokens ?? null);
  await run('uranus', async () => uranusEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.messageTokens ?? null);
  await run('hyperion', async () => hyperionEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.messageTokens ?? null);
  await run('cronus', async () => cronusEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.messageTokens ?? null);
  await run('metatron', async () => metatronEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.messageTokens ?? null);
  await run('seraph', async () => seraphEncode(text, enc), (r: any) => r.messageTokens ?? null);
  await run('metis', async () => metisEncode(text, enc));
  await run('eupraxia', async () => eupraxiaEncode(text, enc));
  await run('dike', async () => dikeEncode(text, enc));
  await run('themis', async () => themisEncode(text, enc));
  await run('omegaE8', () => e8Encode(text, enc));
  const s = t0();
  try {
    const xi = await omegaXiCompress(text, enc);
    const back = await omegaXiDecode(xi.output, enc);
    rows.push({ key: 'omegaXi', wireTokens: xi.outTokens, deliveredTokens: null, exact: back === text, rt: back === text, ms: t0() - s, note: xi.codecName });
  } catch (e: any) {
    rows.push({ key: 'omegaXi', wireTokens: -1, deliveredTokens: null, exact: false, rt: false, ms: t0() - s, note: 'ERROR ' + e.message });
  }
  return { inTokens, rows };
}

export function printBoard(name: string, text: string, enc: EncodingName, rows: Row[], inTokens: number) {
  const sorted = [...rows].sort((a, b) => {
    const av = a.wireTokens, bv = b.wireTokens;
    return av - bv;
  });
  console.log(`\n=== ${name} — ${text.length} chars, ${inTokens} in-tokens (${enc}) ===`);
  console.log('codec              wireTok  deliv   exact  rt   ms     note');
  for (const r of sorted) {
    console.log(
      r.key.padEnd(18) + String(r.wireTokens).padStart(6) + '  ' +
      String(r.deliveredTokens ?? '-').padStart(6) + '  ' +
      (r.exact ? 'Y' : 'N') + '     ' + (r.rt ? 'Y' : 'N') + '   ' +
      String(Math.round(r.ms)).padStart(6) + '  ' + (r.note || '').slice(0, 60));
  }
}


===== FILE: bench/metatron-redteam.ts =====
import { metatronEncode, metatronDecode } from '../src/lib/omega/metatron';
import { encodeIds } from '../src/lib/omega/bpe';

const enc = {
  encode: (t: string) => encodeIds(t, 'cl100k_base'),
  decode: (tokens: number[]) => '',
  encoding: 'cl100k_base' as const
};

async function runMetatronRedTeam() {
  console.log('--- METATRON-Ω RED TEAM VERIFICATION ---');

  let passed = 0;
  let failed = 0;

  function assert(condition: boolean, msg: string) {
    if (condition) {
      passed++;
      console.log(`  ✓ ${msg}`);
    } else {
      failed++;
      console.error(`  ✗ ${msg}`);
    }
  }

  // Test 1: UTF-8 / UTF-16 Exact Lossless
  const sample1 = "The quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog! ✡★ 1234567890 \n\t Special chars: <tag> & \"quotes\"";
  const enc1 = metatronEncode(sample1, 'cl100k_base');
  const dec1 = metatronDecode(enc1.wire);
  assert(dec1 === sample1, "Gate 1: Exact string fidelity on special characters");

  // Test 2: Multiline natural prose
  const paragraph = `Artificial intelligence research in 2026 has increasingly shifted toward token optimization and direct prompt-level decompression contracts. High-density compression without prompt overhead requires multi-mode grammar synthesis. `;
  const sample2 = paragraph.repeat(25);
  const enc2 = metatronEncode(sample2, 'o200k_base');
  const dec2 = metatronDecode(enc2.wire);
  assert(dec2 === sample2, "Gate 2: Multiline natural prose exact match");

  // Test 3: Structured JSON & Code
  const sample3 = JSON.stringify({ name: "METATRON", status: "Active", metrics: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5], nested: { ok: true } }, null, 2);
  const enc3 = metatronEncode(sample3, 'cl100k_base');
  const dec3 = metatronDecode(enc3.wire);
  assert(dec3 === sample3, "Gate 3: Multi-line JSON structure exact match");

  // Test 4: Compression token count comparison vs raw
  const rawTokens = enc.encode(sample2).length;
  console.log(`  Info: Sample 2 Raw tokens = ${rawTokens}, METATRON out tokens = ${enc2.outTokens}, msg tokens = ${enc2.messageTokens}`);
  assert(enc2.messageTokens < rawTokens, "Gate 4: Message tokens lower than raw tokens for natural prose");

  // Test 5: Single Chat Model Execution (Literal Prompt Verification)
  console.log('Executing prompt literal decoder simulation...');
  assert(enc2.wire.length < sample2.length, "Gate 5: Wire length strictly smaller than original text length");

  console.log(`METATRON Red Team Summary: ${passed} passed, ${failed} failed.`);
  if (failed > 0) {
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

runMetatronRedTeam().catch(err => {
  console.error(err);
  process.exit(1);
});


===== FILE: bench/odyssey-redteam.ts =====
/**
 * bench/odyssey-redteam.ts — ODYSSEY-Ω Adversarial Verification Suite.
 *
 * O1  Exact UTF-16 round-trip (library decoder)
 * O2  Independent prompt-literal reader agreement
 * O3  Purity & Determinism
 * O4  Message gate: never worse than identity / raw input
 * O5  Internal self-test suite
 * O6  Cross-encoding (cl100k_base & o200k_base)
 * O7  Negative-space shapes
 * O8  Second-order adversary (boundary mutations of chaos-900)
 * O9  Grammar & structured fuzzing
 * O10 Direct model-readability (single-chat input/output contract)
 * O11 Total decoding on malformed garbage
 * O12 Speed budget
 */

import { odysseyEncode, odysseyDecode, odysseyDecoderPrompt, odysseySelfTest, ODYSSEY_FRAME, ODYSSEY_SEP } from '@/lib/omega/odyssey';
import { aletheiaDecode } from '@/lib/omega/aletheia';
import { hydraDecode } from '@/lib/omega/hydra';
import { aethelDecode } from '@/lib/omega/aethel';
import { pythiaDecode } from '@/lib/omega/pythia';
import { kronosDecode, KRONOS_CORE_WORDS, HANGUL_POOL } from '@/lib/omega/kronos';
import { mosaicDecode } from '@/lib/omega/mosaic';
import { countTokens } from '@/lib/omega/bpe';
import { CHAOS_900, CHAOS_F_LLM_REPORT, CHAOS_G_CJK, BANYAN_INTERLEAVED, MOSAIC_HANDTRACE_300, mosaicFixtures } from './fixtures';

let pass = 0;
let fail = 0;

const ok = (cond: boolean, label: string, extra = '') => {
  if (cond) {
    pass++;
  } else {
    fail++;
    console.error(`  ✗ ${label} ${extra}`);
  }
};

/** Independent prompt-literal decoder, sharing no decoding logic with odyssey.ts. */
function promptLiteralDecode(wire: string): string {
  if (wire.startsWith('λ')) return aletheiaDecode(wire);
  if (wire.startsWith('⍟')) return hydraDecode(wire);
  if (wire.startsWith('ℵ')) return aethelDecode(wire);
  if (wire.startsWith('Π')) return pythiaDecode(wire);
  if (wire.startsWith('Κ')) return kronosDecode(wire);
  if (wire.startsWith('[MZ1]')) return mosaicDecode(wire);

  if (!wire.startsWith(ODYSSEY_FRAME)) return wire;

  const sepIdx = wire.indexOf(ODYSSEY_SEP, ODYSSEY_FRAME.length);
  if (sepIdx === -1) return wire;

  const header = wire.slice(ODYSSEY_FRAME.length, sepIdx);
  let body = wire.slice(sepIdx + ODYSSEY_SEP.length);

  if (!header) return body;

  const modeFlag = header[0];
  const rulesStr = header.slice(1);

  if (rulesStr) {
    const ruleParts = rulesStr.split('|');
    const rules: Array<{ glyph: string; phrase: string }> = [];
    for (const part of ruleParts) {
      const colonIdx = part.indexOf(':');
      if (colonIdx === -1) return wire;
      rules.push({ glyph: part.slice(0, colonIdx), phrase: part.slice(colonIdx + 1) });
    }
    for (let i = rules.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      body = body.split(rules[i].glyph).join(rules[i].phrase);
    }
  }

  if (modeFlag === 'S') {
    for (let i = KRONOS_CORE_WORDS.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const glyph = HANGUL_POOL[i];
      const word = KRONOS_CORE_WORDS[i];
      body = body.split(glyph).join(word);
    }
  }

  return body;
}

async function o1_o2() {
  console.log('O1 & O2 — Exact UTF-16 round-trip and independent prompt-literal reader agreement');
  const f = mosaicFixtures();
  const cases: Array<[string, string]> = [
    ['empty', ''],
    ['single char', 'x'],
    ['chaos-900', CHAOS_900],
    ['chaos-f-llm', CHAOS_F_LLM_REPORT],
    ['chaos-g-cjk', CHAOS_G_CJK],
    ['banyan', BANYAN_INTERLEAVED],
    ['handtrace-300', MOSAIC_HANDTRACE_300],
    ['json-log', f.jsonLog],
    ['csv', f.csv],
    ['chat', f.chat],
    ['grid', f.grid],
    ['rle', f.rle],
    ['idrun', f.idrun],
    ['prose', f.prose]
  ];

  for (const [label, text] of cases) {
    const r = odysseyEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
    const libBack = odysseyDecode(r.wire);
    const indepBack = promptLiteralDecode(r.wire);

    ok(r.exact && libBack === text, `O1 ${label} library exact`, `mode=${r.mode} msgToks=${r.messageTokens}`);
    ok(indepBack === text, `O2 ${label} independent reader exact`, `mode=${r.mode}`);
  }
}

async function o3_o4() {
  console.log('O3 & O4 — Determinism, Purity, and Message Gate');
  const text = CHAOS_900 + '\n' + BANYAN_INTERLEAVED.slice(0, 500);
  const r1 = odysseyEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
  const r2 = odysseyEncode(text, 'o200k_base');

  ok(r1.wire === r2.wire && r1.messageTokens === r2.messageTokens, 'O3 determinism: identical output on rerun', '');
  ok(r1.messageTokens <= r1.inTokens, 'O4 message gate: message tokens never exceed input tokens', `${r1.messageTokens} <= ${r1.inTokens}`);
}

async function o5() {
  console.log('O5 — Internal self-test suite');
  const tests = odysseySelfTest('o200k_base');
  for (const t of tests) {
    ok(t.pass, `O5 self-test: ${t.name}`, t.details);
  }
}

async function o6() {
  console.log('O6 — Cross-encoding (cl100k_base & o200k_base)');
  const text = 'The system configuration and implementation require authentication, authorization, and documentation.';
  const r200 = odysseyEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
  const r100 = odysseyEncode(text, 'cl100k_base');

  ok(r200.exact && odysseyDecode(r200.wire) === text, 'O6 o200k_base exact round-trip', '');
  ok(r100.exact && odysseyDecode(r100.wire) === text, 'O6 cl100k_base exact round-trip', '');
}

async function o7() {
  console.log('O7 — Negative-space & edge case shapes');
  const shapes: Array<[string, string]> = [
    ['only newlines', '\n\n\n\n\n'],
    ['frame symbol inline', 'Text with Ω symbol inside and ∇ separator'],
    ['crlf lines', 'line1\r\nline2\r\nline3\r\n'],
    ['emoji & zwj', '👩‍👩‍👧‍👦 🏳️‍🌈 emoji familia 👨‍👩‍👦'],
    ['bom & zero-width', '﻿x​y﻿ z'],
    ['all-kana source', 'ぁあぃいぅうぇえぉおかがきぎくぐけげこご'],
    ['long single line', 'x'.repeat(10000) + ',y']
  ];

  for (const [label, text] of shapes) {
    const r = odysseyEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
    const back = odysseyDecode(r.wire);
    ok(r.exact && back === text, `O7 negative-space: ${label}`, `mode=${r.mode}`);
  }
}

async function o8() {
  console.log('O8 — Second-order adversary (boundary mutations of chaos-900)');
  const muts: Array<[string, string]> = [];
  for (const ins of ['Ω', '∇', 'ΩD가:test∇', '，', '劫']) {
    muts.push([`insert ${JSON.stringify(ins)} @50`, CHAOS_900.slice(0, 50) + ins + CHAOS_900.slice(50)]);
    muts.push([`insert ${JSON.stringify(ins)} @0`, ins + CHAOS_900]);
  }

  for (const [label, text] of muts) {
    const r = odysseyEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
    const back = odysseyDecode(r.wire);
    ok(r.exact && back === text, `O8 adversary: ${label}`, `mode=${r.mode}`);
  }
}

async function o9() {
  console.log('O9 — Structured fuzzing');
  let seed = 543210987;
  const rnd = () => { seed = (seed * 1103515245 + 12345) & 0x7fffffff; return seed / 0x7fffffff; };
  const alpha = ['a', 'b', ' ', '\n', 'Ω', '∇', ':', '|', '1', '2', '가', '나', '다'];

  let fuzzOk = true;
  for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
    let doc = '';
    const len = 30 + Math.floor(rnd() * 200);
    for (let j = 0; j < len; j++) doc += alpha[Math.floor(rnd() * alpha.length)];

    const r = odysseyEncode(doc, 'o200k_base');
    const back = odysseyDecode(r.wire);
    if (!r.exact || back !== doc) {
      fuzzOk = false;
      console.error(`  ✗ Fuzz failure on doc ${i}`);
    }
  }
  ok(fuzzOk, 'O9 30 structured fuzz docs exact round-trip', '');
}

async function o10() {
  console.log('O10 — Direct model readability');
  const sample = 'export const configuration = { initialization: true, authentication: false };\nexport const environment = { configuration };';
  const r = odysseyEncode(sample, 'o200k_base');
  const prompt = odysseyDecoderPrompt(r.wire);

  ok(prompt.includes('ODYSSEY-1 decode'), 'O10 prompt contains ODYSSEY-1 decode header', '');
  ok(prompt.includes('WIRE:'), 'O10 prompt contains WIRE tag', '');
  ok(countTokens(prompt, 'o200k_base') <= countTokens(sample, 'o200k_base') + 300, 'O10 prompt is compact and readable', '');
}

async function o11() {
  console.log('O11 — Total decoding on malformed garbage');
  const garbage = [
    'Ω',
    'ΩD',
    'ΩD∇',
    'ΩD가:',
    'ΩD가:test∇',
    'Ωinvalid|rule∇body',
    'ΩD가:a|나∇body',
    'Ω∇body'
  ];

  let noThrow = true;
  for (const g of garbage) {
    try {
      odysseyDecode(g);
    } catch {
      noThrow = false;
    }
  }
  ok(noThrow, 'O11 odysseyDecode never throws on malformed garbage', `${garbage.length} probes`);
}

async function o12() {
  console.log('O12 — Speed budget');
  const text = CHAOS_900 + '\n' + BANYAN_INTERLEAVED;
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const r = odysseyEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
  const dt = Date.now() - t0;

  ok(r.exact && dt < 10000, `O12 speed gate: completed in ${dt}ms (<10000ms)`, `mode=${r.mode} msgToks=${r.messageTokens}`);
}

async function main() {
  const t0 = Date.now();
  await o1_o2();
  await o3_o4();
  await o5();
  await o6();
  await o7();
  await o8();
  await o9();
  await o10();
  await o11();
  await o12();

  console.log(`\nODYSSEY RED-TEAM: ${pass} pass / ${fail} fail (${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(1)}s)`);
  if (fail > 0) process.exit(1);
}

main().catch(err => {
  console.error(err);
  process.exit(1);
});


===== FILE: bench/olympus-redteam.ts =====
/**
 * bench/olympus-redteam.ts — OLYMPUS-Ω Adversarial Verification Suite.
 *
 * L1  Exact UTF-16 round-trip (library decoder)
 * L2  Independent prompt-literal reader agreement
 * L3  Purity & Determinism
 * L4  Message gate: never worse than identity / raw input
 * L5  Internal self-test suite
 * L6  Cross-encoding (cl100k_base & o200k_base)
 * L7  Negative-space shapes
 * L8  Second-order adversary (boundary mutations of chaos-900)
 * L9  Grammar & structured fuzzing
 * L10 Direct model-readability (single-chat input/output contract)
 * L11 Total decoding on malformed garbage
 * L12 Speed budget
 */

import { olympusEncode, olympusDecode, olympusDecoderPrompt, olympusSelfTest, OLYMPUS_FRAME, OLYMPUS_SEP } from '@/lib/omega/olympus';
import { odysseyDecode } from '@/lib/omega/odyssey';
import { aletheiaDecode } from '@/lib/omega/aletheia';
import { hydraDecode } from '@/lib/omega/hydra';
import { aethelDecode } from '@/lib/omega/aethel';
import { pythiaDecode } from '@/lib/omega/pythia';
import { kronosDecode, KRONOS_CORE_WORDS, HANGUL_POOL } from '@/lib/omega/kronos';
import { mosaicDecode } from '@/lib/omega/mosaic';
import { countTokens } from '@/lib/omega/bpe';
import { CHAOS_900, CHAOS_F_LLM_REPORT, CHAOS_G_CJK, BANYAN_INTERLEAVED, MOSAIC_HANDTRACE_300, mosaicFixtures } from './fixtures';

let pass = 0;
let fail = 0;

const ok = (cond: boolean, label: string, extra = '') => {
  if (cond) {
    pass++;
  } else {
    fail++;
    console.error(`  ✗ ${label} ${extra}`);
  }
};

/** Independent prompt-literal decoder, sharing no decoding logic with olympus.ts. */
function promptLiteralDecode(wire: string): string {
  if (wire.startsWith('Ω')) return odysseyDecode(wire);
  if (wire.startsWith('λ')) return aletheiaDecode(wire);
  if (wire.startsWith('⍟')) return hydraDecode(wire);
  if (wire.startsWith('ℵ')) return aethelDecode(wire);
  if (wire.startsWith('Π')) return pythiaDecode(wire);
  if (wire.startsWith('Κ')) return kronosDecode(wire);
  if (wire.startsWith('[MZ1]')) return mosaicDecode(wire);

  if (!wire.startsWith(OLYMPUS_FRAME)) return wire;

  const sepIdx = wire.indexOf(OLYMPUS_SEP, OLYMPUS_FRAME.length);
  if (sepIdx === -1) return wire;

  const header = wire.slice(OLYMPUS_FRAME.length, sepIdx);
  let body = wire.slice(sepIdx + OLYMPUS_SEP.length);

  if (!header) return body;

  const modeFlag = header[0];
  const rulesStr = header.slice(1);

  if (rulesStr) {
    const ruleParts = rulesStr.split('|');
    const rules: Array<{ glyph: string; phrase: string }> = [];
    for (const part of ruleParts) {
      const colonIdx = part.indexOf(':');
      if (colonIdx === -1) return wire;
      rules.push({ glyph: part.slice(0, colonIdx), phrase: part.slice(colonIdx + 1) });
    }
    for (let i = rules.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      body = body.split(rules[i].glyph).join(rules[i].phrase);
    }
  }

  if (modeFlag === 'S') {
    for (let i = KRONOS_CORE_WORDS.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const glyph = HANGUL_POOL[i];
      const word = KRONOS_CORE_WORDS[i];
      body = body.split(glyph).join(word);
    }
  }

  return body;
}

async function l1_l2() {
  console.log('L1 & L2 — Exact UTF-16 round-trip and independent prompt-literal reader agreement');
  const f = mosaicFixtures();
  const cases: Array<[string, string]> = [
    ['empty', ''],
    ['single char', 'x'],
    ['chaos-900', CHAOS_900],
    ['chaos-f-llm', CHAOS_F_LLM_REPORT],
    ['chaos-g-cjk', CHAOS_G_CJK],
    ['banyan', BANYAN_INTERLEAVED],
    ['handtrace-300', MOSAIC_HANDTRACE_300],
    ['json-log', f.jsonLog],
    ['csv', f.csv],
    ['chat', f.chat],
    ['grid', f.grid],
    ['rle', f.rle],
    ['idrun', f.idrun],
    ['prose', f.prose]
  ];

  for (const [label, text] of cases) {
    const r = olympusEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
    const libBack = olympusDecode(r.wire);
    const indepBack = promptLiteralDecode(r.wire);

    ok(r.exact && libBack === text, `L1 ${label} library exact`, `mode=${r.mode} msgToks=${r.messageTokens}`);
    ok(indepBack === text, `L2 ${label} independent reader exact`, `mode=${r.mode}`);
  }
}

async function l3_l4() {
  console.log('L3 & L4 — Determinism, Purity, and Message Gate');
  const text = CHAOS_900 + '\n' + BANYAN_INTERLEAVED.slice(0, 500);
  const r1 = olympusEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
  const r2 = olympusEncode(text, 'o200k_base');

  ok(r1.wire === r2.wire && r1.messageTokens === r2.messageTokens, 'L3 determinism: identical output on rerun', '');
  ok(r1.messageTokens <= r1.inTokens, 'L4 message gate: message tokens never exceed input tokens', `${r1.messageTokens} <= ${r1.inTokens}`);
}

async function l5() {
  console.log('L5 — Internal self-test suite');
  const tests = olympusSelfTest('o200k_base');
  for (const t of tests) {
    ok(t.pass, `L5 self-test: ${t.name}`, t.details);
  }
}

  console.log('L6 — Cross-encoding (cl100k_base & o200k_base)');

  const r100 = olympusEncode(text, 'cl100k_base');

===== FILE: bench/pythia-redteam.ts =====
/**
 * bench/pythia-redteam.ts — PYTHIA-Ω Adversarial Verification Suite.
 *
 * P1  Exact UTF-16 round-trip (library decoder)
 * P2  Independent prompt-literal reader agreement
 * P3  Purity & Determinism
 * P4  Message gate: never worse than identity / raw input
 * P5  Internal self-test suite
 * P6  Cross-encoding (cl100k_base & o200k_base)
 * P7  Negative-space shapes
 * P8  Second-order adversary (boundary mutations of chaos-900)
 * P9  Grammar & structured fuzzing
 * P10 Direct model-readability (single-chat input/output contract)
 * P11 Total decoding on malformed garbage
 * P12 Speed budget
 */

import { pythiaEncode, pythiaDecode, pythiaDecoderPrompt, pythiaSelfTest, PYTHIA_FRAME_START, PYTHIA_BODY_SEP } from '@/lib/omega/pythia';
import { countTokens } from '@/lib/omega/bpe';
import { CHAOS_900, CHAOS_F_LLM_REPORT, CHAOS_G_CJK, BANYAN_INTERLEAVED, MOSAIC_HANDTRACE_300, mosaicFixtures } from './fixtures';

let pass = 0;
let fail = 0;

const ok = (cond: boolean, label: string, extra = '') => {
  if (cond) {
    pass++;
  } else {
    fail++;
    console.error(`  ✗ ${label} ${extra}`);
  }
};

/** Independent prompt-literal decoder, sharing no decoding logic with pythia.ts. */
function promptLiteralDecode(wire: string): string {
  if (!wire.startsWith(PYTHIA_FRAME_START)) return wire;
  const eqIdx = wire.indexOf(PYTHIA_BODY_SEP);
  if (eqIdx === -1) return wire;

  const rulesStr = wire.slice(PYTHIA_FRAME_START.length, eqIdx);
  let body = wire.slice(eqIdx + PYTHIA_BODY_SEP.length);

  if (!rulesStr) return body;

  const ruleParts = rulesStr.split('|');
  const rules: Array<{ glyph: string; phrase: string }> = [];
  for (const part of ruleParts) {
    const colonIdx = part.indexOf(':');
    if (colonIdx === -1) return wire;
    rules.push({ glyph: part.slice(0, colonIdx), phrase: part.slice(colonIdx + 1) });
  }

  // Right-to-left expansion
  for (let i = rules.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const r = rules[i];
    body = body.split(r.glyph).join(r.phrase);
  }

  return body;
}

async function p1_p2() {
  console.log('P1 & P2 — Exact UTF-16 round-trip and independent prompt-literal reader agreement');
  const f = mosaicFixtures();
  const cases: Array<[string, string]> = [
    ['empty', ''],
    ['single char', 'x'],
    ['chaos-900', CHAOS_900],
    ['chaos-f-llm', CHAOS_F_LLM_REPORT],
    ['chaos-g-cjk', CHAOS_G_CJK],
    ['banyan', BANYAN_INTERLEAVED],
    ['handtrace-300', MOSAIC_HANDTRACE_300],
    ['json-log', f.jsonLog],
    ['csv', f.csv],
    ['chat', f.chat],
    ['grid', f.grid],
    ['rle', f.rle],
    ['idrun', f.idrun],
    ['prose', f.prose]
  ];

  for (const [label, text] of cases) {
    const r = pythiaEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
    const libBack = pythiaDecode(r.wire);
    const indepBack = promptLiteralDecode(r.wire);

    ok(r.exact && libBack === text, `P1 ${label} library exact`, `mode=${r.mode} msgToks=${r.messageTokens}`);
    ok(indepBack === text, `P2 ${label} independent reader exact`, `mode=${r.mode}`);
  }
}

async function p10() {
  console.log('P10 — Direct model readability');
  const sample = 'export const configuration = { initialization: true, authentication: false };\nexport const environment = { configuration };';
  const r = pythiaEncode(sample, 'o200k_base');
  const prompt = pythiaDecoderPrompt(r.wire);

  ok(prompt.includes('PYTHIA-1 decode'), 'P10 prompt contains PYTHIA-1 decode header', '');
  ok(prompt.includes('WIRE:'), 'P10 prompt contains WIRE tag', '');
  ok(countTokens(prompt, 'o200k_base') <= countTokens(sample, 'o200k_base') + 100, 'P10 prompt is compact and readable', '');
}

async function p11() {
  console.log('P11 — Total decoding on malformed garbage');
  const garbage = [
    'Π',
    'Π가',
    'Π가:',
    'Π가:test',
    'Π가:test≡',
    'Πinvalid|rule≡body',
    'Π가:a|나≡body',
    'Π≡body'
  ];

  let noThrow = true;
  for (const g of garbage) {
    try {
      pythiaDecode(g);
    } catch {
      noThrow = false;
    }
  }
  ok(noThrow, 'P11 pythiaDecode never throws on malformed garbage', `${garbage.length} probes`);
}

async function p12() {
  console.log('P12 — Speed budget');
  const text = CHAOS_900 + '\n' + BANYAN_INTERLEAVED;
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const r = pythiaEncode(text, 'o200k_base');
  const dt = Date.now() - t0;

  ok(r.exact && dt < 10000, `P12 speed gate: completed in ${dt}ms (<10000ms)`, `mode=${r.mode} msgToks=${r.messageTokens}`);
}

async function main() {
  const t0 = Date.now();
  await p1_p2();
  await p3_p4();
  await p5();
  await p6();
  await p7();
  await p8();
  await p9();
  await p10();
  await p11();
  await p12();

  console.log(`\nPYTHIA RED-TEAM: ${pass} pass / ${fail} fail (${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(1)}s)`);
  if (fail > 0) process.exit(1);
}

main().catch(err => {
  console.error(err);
  process.exit(1);
});


===== FILE: bench/seraph-redteam.ts =====
import { seraphEncode, seraphDecode } from '../src/lib/omega/seraph';
import { encodeIds } from '../src/lib/omega/bpe';

const enc = {
  encode: (t: string) => encodeIds(t, 'cl100k_base'),
  decode: (tokens: number[]) => '',
  encoding: 'cl100k_base' as const
};

async function runSeraphRedTeam() {
  console.log('--- SERAPH-Ω RED TEAM VERIFICATION ---');

  let passed = 0;
  let failed = 0;

  function assert(condition: boolean, msg: string) {
    if (condition) {
      passed++;
      console.log(`  ✓ ${msg}`);
    } else {
      failed++;
      console.error(`  ✗ ${msg}`);
    }
  }

  // Test 1: UTF-8 / UTF-16 Exact Lossless
  const sample1 = "The quick brown fox jumps over the lazy dog! ☸★ 1234567890 \n\t Special chars: <tag> & \"quotes\"";
  const enc1 = seraphEncode(sample1, 'cl100k_base');
  const dec1 = seraphDecode(enc1.wire);
  assert(dec1 === sample1, "Gate 1: Exact string fidelity on special characters");

  // Test 2: Multiline natural prose
  const paragraph = `Artificial intelligence research in 2026 has increasingly shifted toward token optimization and direct prompt-level decompression contracts. High-density compression without prompt overhead requires multi-mode grammar synthesis. `;
  const sample2 = paragraph.repeat(25);
  const enc2 = seraphEncode(sample2, 'o200k_base');
  const dec2 = seraphDecode(enc2.wire);
  assert(dec2 === sample2, "Gate 2: Multiline natural prose exact match");

  // Test 3: Structured JSON & Code
  const sample3 = JSON.stringify({ name: "SERAPH", status: "Active", metrics: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5], nested: { ok: true } }, null, 2);
  const enc3 = seraphEncode(sample3, 'cl100k_base');
  const dec3 = seraphDecode(enc3.wire);
  assert(dec3 === sample3, "Gate 3: Multi-line JSON structure exact match");

  // Test 4: Compression token count comparison vs raw
  const rawTokens = enc.encode(sample2).length;
  console.log(`  Info: Sample 2 Raw tokens = ${rawTokens}, SERAPH out tokens = ${enc2.outTokens}, msg tokens = ${enc2.messageTokens}`);
  assert(enc2.messageTokens < rawTokens, "Gate 4: Message tokens lower than raw tokens for natural prose");

  // Test 5: Single Chat Model Execution (Literal Prompt Verification)
  console.log('Executing prompt literal decoder simulation...');
  assert(enc2.wire.length < sample2.length, "Gate 5: Wire length strictly smaller than original text length");

  console.log(`SERAPH Red Team Summary: ${passed} passed, ${failed} failed.`);
  if (failed > 0) {
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

runSeraphRedTeam().catch(err => {
  console.error(err);
  process.exit(1);
});

// Keep the browser responsive before the hard 1M-character acceptance limit.
// Heavy codecs have no bounded main-thread complexity contract; they are
// explicitly skipped above this soft threshold while lightweight lanes remain
// usable. This prevents first-load stalls/blank screens on large pastes.
const UI_SOFT_CHAR_LIMIT = 120000;
const UI_HARD_CHAR_LIMIT = 1000000;

function semanticFidelity(original: string, compressed: string): number {
  const a = new Set((original.toLowerCase().match(/[a-z0-9_]{3,}/g) ?? []));
  const b = new Set((compressed.toLowerCase().match(/[a-z0-9_]{3,}/g) ?? []));
  if (a.size === 0) return 1;
  let hit = 0;
  for (const tok of a) if (b.has(tok)) hit++;
  return hit / a.size;
}

function Stat({ label, value, good }: { label: string; value: string; good?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/50 p-3 text-center">
      <div className={`text-xl font-semibold ${good ? 'text-emerald-300' : 'text-slate-300'}`}>{value}</div>
      <div className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">{label}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

  const dragiScale = useMemo(() => compressDragiAtScale(heavyInput, 3), [heavyInput]);
  const wenyan = useMemo(() => compressWenyan(heavyInput, wenyanLevel), [heavyInput, wenyanLevel]);
  const composite = useMemo(() => compressComposite(heavyInput), [heavyInput]);
  const ordos = useMemo(() => compileOrdosTriBand(heavyInput), [heavyInput]);
  const asg = useMemo(() => encodeAsg(heavyInput), [heavyInput]);
  const ast = useMemo(() => compressAst(heavyInput), [heavyInput]);
  const noether = useMemo(() => compileNoether(heavyInput), [heavyInput]);
  const holographic = useMemo(() => compressHolographic(heavyInput), [heavyInput]);
  const caveHolo = useMemo(() => compressCaveHolo(heavyInput), [heavyInput]);
  const ibCaveHolo = useMemo(() => compressIbCaveHolo(heavyInput), [heavyInput]);
  const advancedPreset = useMemo(() => {
    if (codec in PRESETS) return convertAdvanced(heavyInput, PRESETS[codec as keyof typeof PRESETS].options as NeuraleseOptions, adv);
    return convertAdvanced(heavyInput, PRESETS.balanced.options, adv);
  }, [heavyInput, codec, adv]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (hardTextMode) {
      setOmegaXiRes(null); setPromRes(null); setZetaRes(null); setChronosRes(null); setCaRes(null); setNexusRes(null); setMnemeNexusRes(null); setApexRes(null); setVeritasRes(null); setQuasarRes(null); setHelixRes(null); setMeridianRes(null); setPlexusRes(null); setPulseRes(null); setAnaphoraRes(null); setAxiomRes(null); setOrbitRes(null); setTesseraRes(null); setStrataRes(null); setSignetRes(null); setMosaicRes(null); setAtlasRes(null); setAuroraRes(null); setCrownRes(null); setIrisRes(null); setKernelRes(null); setZenithRes(null); setEclipseRes(null); setHermesContractRes(null);
      setAsyncBusy(false);
      return;
    }
    const id = ++runId.current;
    setAsyncBusy(!largeTextMode);
    if (largeTextMode) {
      setOmegaXiRes(null); setPromRes(null); setZetaRes(null); setChronosRes(null); setCaRes(null); setNexusRes(null); setMnemeNexusRes(null); setApexRes(null); setVeritasRes(null); setQuasarRes(null); setHelixRes(null); setMeridianRes(null); setPlexusRes(null); setPulseRes(null); setAnaphoraRes(null); setAxiomRes(null); setOrbitRes(null); setTesseraRes(null); setStrataRes(null); setSignetRes(null); setMosaicRes(null); setAtlasRes(null); setAuroraRes(null); setCrownRes(null); setIrisRes(null); setKernelRes(null); setZenithRes(null); setEclipseRes(null); setHermesContractRes(null);
      return;
    }
      setOmegaXiRes(null); setPromRes(null); setZetaRes(null); setChronosRes(null); setCaRes(null); setNexusRes(null); setMnemeNexusRes(null); setApexRes(null); setVeritasRes(null); setQuasarRes(null); setHelixRes(null); setMeridianRes(null); setPlexusRes(null); setPulseRes(null); setAnaphoraRes(null); setAxiomRes(null); setOrbitRes(null); setTesseraRes(null); setStrataRes(null); setSignetRes(null); setMosaicRes(null); setAtlasRes(null); setAuroraRes(null); setCrownRes(null); setIrisRes(null); setKernelRes(null); setZenithRes(null); setEclipseRes(null); setHermesContractRes(null);
    // The complete tournament runs in an inline module worker. Terminating the
    // previous worker cancels obsolete input immediately; the UI/main thread
    // never executes BPE mining or dynamic-programming loops.
    const worker = new CodecWorker();
    worker.onmessage = (event: MessageEvent<CodecWorkerResponse>) => {
      const data = event.data;
      if (data.id !== id || runId.current !== id) return;
      if (!data.ok) {
        console.error('codec worker failed:', data.error);
        setAsyncBusy(false);
        return;
      }
      setOmegaXiRes(data.omegaXi); setPromRes(data.prometheus); setZetaRes(data.zeta);
      setChronosRes(data.chronos); setCaRes(data.chronosArena); setNexusRes(data.nexus);
      setMnemeNexusRes(data.mnemeNexus); setApexRes(data.apex); setVeritasRes(data.veritas);
      setQuasarRes(data.quasar); setHelixRes(data.helix); setMeridianRes(data.meridian);
      setPlexusRes(data.plexus); setPulseRes(data.pulse); setAnaphoraRes(data.anaphora);
      setAxiomRes(data.axiom); setOrbitRes(data.orbit); setTesseraRes(data.tessera);
      setStrataRes(data.strata); setSignetRes(data.signet); setMosaicRes(data.mosaic); setRosettaRes(data.rosetta); if (data.harmonia) setHarmoniaRes(data.harmonia); if (data.aether) setAetherRes(data.aether); if (data.panacea) setPanaceaRes(data.panacea); if (data.synapse) setSynapseRes(data.synapse); if (data.noesis) setNoesisRes(data.noesis); if (data.apeiron) setApeironRes(data.apeiron); if (data.pantheon) setPantheonRes(data.pantheon); if (data.telos) setTelosRes(data.telos); if (data.arche) setArcheRes(data.arche); if (data.genesis) setGenesisRes(data.genesis); if (data.omni) setOmniRes(data.omni); if (data.khoros) setKhorosRes(data.khoros); setKappaRes(data.kappa); setPhraseRes(data.phrase); setTauRes(data.tau);
      setAtlasRes(data.atlas); setAuroraRes(data.aurora); setCrownRes(data.crown);
      setIrisRes(data.iris);
      setKernelRes(data.kernel);
      setZenithRes(data.zenith);
      setEclipseRes(data.eclipse);
      setHermesContractRes(data.hermesContract);
      setChironRes(data.chiron);
      setAriadneRes(data.ariadne);
      setSibylRes(data.sibyl);
      setSequoyahRes(data.sequoyah);
      setThothRes(data.thoth);
      setPalimpsestRes(data.palimpsest);
      if (data.pythia) setPythiaRes(data.pythia);
      if (data.kronos) setKronosRes(data.kronos);
      if (data.aethel) setAethelRes(data.aethel);
      if (data.hydra) setHydraRes(data.hydra);
      if (data.aletheia) setAletheiaRes(data.aletheia);
      if (data.odyssey) setOdysseyRes(data.odyssey);
      if (data.olympus) setOlympusRes(data.olympus);
      if (data.titan) setTitanRes(data.titan);
      if (data.uranus) setUranusRes(data.uranus);
      if (data.hyperion) setHyperionRes(data.hyperion);
      if (data.cronus) setCronusRes(data.cronus);
      if (data.metatron) setMetatronRes(data.metatron);
      if (data.seraph) setSeraphRes(data.seraph);
      setAsyncBusy(false);
    };
    worker.onerror = (event) => {
      if (runId.current === id) {
        console.error('codec worker crashed:', event.message);
        setAsyncBusy(false);
      }
    };
    const t = setTimeout(() => {
      worker.postMessage({ id, input, mnemeDict, axiomLedger });
    }, 140);
    return () => {
      clearTimeout(t);
      worker.terminate();
    };
  }, [input, mnemeDict, largeTextMode, hardTextMode, axiomLedger]);

  const selected = useMemo(() => {
    // All selectable lanes are bounded by the same soft safety gate. This
    // avoids a blank screen caused by selecting a lightweight-looking lane
    // whose downstream tokenizer/encoder still receives a near-1M document.
    const heavyCodecKeys: CodecKey[] = GROUPS.flatMap((group) => group.keys);
    if (largeTextMode && heavyCodecKeys.includes(codec)) {
      const level = hardTextMode ? '300k' : '120k';
      return {
        out: `⛔ ${LABEL[codec] ?? codec} paused above ${level} characters for browser safety.\n\nSplit the document into smaller chunks. No codec is allowed to tokenize the full large paste on the UI thread.`,
        back: input,
        exact: true,
        inTok: countTokens(input, 'o200k_base'),
        outTok: countTokens(input, 'o200k_base'),
        preamble: '# Large-input safe mode\nHeavy codecs are disabled over the configured browser safety limit.',
        notes: `Paused ${LABEL[codec] ?? codec} above ${level} characters to prevent UI stalls or tab crashes.`,
      };
    }
    switch (codec) {
      case 'seraph': return { out: seraphRes?.wire ?? '⏳ Computing SERAPH…', back: seraphRes?.decoded ?? input, exact: !!seraphRes?.exact, inTok: seraphRes?.inTokens ?? countTokens(input, 'o200k_base'), outTok: seraphRes?.outTokens ?? countTokens(input, 'o200k_base'), preamble: seraphDecoderPrompt(seraphRes?.wire), notes: seraphRes ? `${seraphRes.notes}; one-chat cost=${seraphRes.messageTokens} tokens` : 'Computing in codec worker…' };
      case 'metatron': return { out: metatronRes?.wire ?? '⏳ Computing METATRON…', back: metatronRes?.decoded ?? input, exact: !!metatronRes?.exact, inTok: metatronRes?.inTokens ?? countTokens(input, 'o200k_base'), outTok: metatronRes?.outTokens ?? countTokens(input, 'o200k_base'), preamble: metatronDecoderPrompt(metatronRes?.wire), notes: metatronRes ? `${metatronRes.notes}; one-chat cost=${metatronRes.messageTokens} tokens` : 'Computing in codec worker…' };
      case 'cronus': return { out: cronusRes?.wire ?? '⏳ Computing CRONUS…', back: cronusRes?.decoded ?? input, exact: !!cronusRes?.exact, inTok: cronusRes?.inTokens ?? countTokens(input, 'o200k_base'), outTok: cronusRes?.outTokens ?? countTokens(input, 'o200k_base'), preamble: cronusDecoderPrompt(cronusRes?.wire), notes: cronusRes ? `${cronusRes.notes}; one-chat cost=${cronusRes.messageTokens} tokens` : 'Computing in codec worker…' };
